operation, and starting without initialization requirements all default to
`false`; interface type `0` selects the instrument's default interface.

Any device IP supplied to these interface commands configures communication
between local SpatialAnalyzer and its instrument. It does not enable remote
Briosa access.

Creates a USMN template for an existing instrument. Overall and component
weights default to `1.0`; moving, the six rigid-body degrees of freedom, and
component weights default enabled, while scale defaults disabled. The exact SDK
argument is misspelled `Overal Instrument Weight`; Briosa uses the correctly
spelled public field while preserving that internal binding.

<summary>SA 2024.1.0508.5: Signature and Disposition</summary>

Planned for the 2024 target; implementation and runtime validation are pending.

The MP inputs, outputs, and choices match the 2026 counterpart. Its disposition rationale also applies to 2024.

**Captured 2024 Argument Signature**

| Direction | Exact MP Argument | Argument Kind |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Input | `Point Name` | Point Name |
| Input | `Observation Index` | Integer |
| Output | `Resulting Instrument` | Collection Instrument ID |
| Output | `Resultant Vector` | Vector |
| Output | `Active?` | Boolean |
| Output | `Timestamp` | String |
| Output | `RMS Error` | Double |
| Output | `Temperature (deg F)` | Double |
| Output | `Pressure (in. Hg)` | Double |
| Output | `Humidity (% RH)` | Double |
| Output | `Info Data` | String |

</details>

<p className="catalog-reference-scope">Reference below: SA 2026.1.0529.7. API links and Briosa defaults apply to that target.</p>

Returns the source instrument, raw distance/azimuth/elevation values, active
status, timestamp string, RMS error, temperature in degrees Fahrenheit,
pressure in inches of mercury, relative humidity percentage, and raw info data
for a zero-based observation index. SA returns `-1` when temperature, pressure,
or humidity is unavailable and `0` when RMS error is unavailable. Briosa does
not normalize instrument-specific angular conventions or parse the timestamp
and info strings.

| Direction | Exact MP Argument | Argument Kind |
| --- | --- | --- |

| Input | `Max Distance` | Double |

</details>

<p className="catalog-reference-scope">Reference below: SA 2026.1.0529.7. API links and Briosa defaults apply to that target.</p>

Adds fabricated observations from a selected instrument to the constructed
points in an SA Point Group. Introducing instrument error and limiting distance
both default to `false`; minimum and maximum distance default to `0.0` and
`1000000.0`. SA ignores the bounds when distance limiting is disabled. Briosa
adds no preflight or transaction.

collimation, baseline determined by value, no moving-instrument azimuth zero,
and baseline distance `0.0`. Scale and known-point inputs apply according to
the selected baseline method. Briosa passes omitted mode-specific points as
empty MP values and leaves workflow validation to SA.

Use the SpatialAnalyzer workflow directly; reconsider the direct RPC when exact-target binding evidence is available.

Collection-object inputs use the [2024 collection/name binding](/mp-command-catalog/2024.1.0508.5/review-notes#collection-object-bindings).

| Input | `Group name` | Collection Object Name |
| Input | `Wait for Completion` | Boolean |

A missing exported binding is recorded explicitly. Existing limitations retain their reviewed handling; a generic SDK method alone does not prove a replacement binding.

</details>

<p className="catalog-reference-scope">Reference below: SA 2026.1.0529.7. API links and Briosa defaults apply to that target.</p>

Scans inside the supplied perimeter list while omitting the supplied exclusion
perimeters. The parameter-set name and destination Point Group are required,
and waiting for completion defaults to `true`. The exact SA 2026.1 SDK export
includes the exclusion-perimeter input even though an older installed argument
table omits it.

point defining the search direction, a parameter set, a destination Point

tracking or prevalidating the workflow state.

Forcing an existing actuals group, showing the complete dialog, and automatic
start all default to `false`; waiting for completion defaults to `true`.
`Show complete dialog? = false` selects SA's abbreviated interface rather than
guaranteeing no UI. Callers can separately lower SA's interactivity level when
they need to suppress that interface; Briosa does not silently change
application-global interactivity for this call.

Point and vector thresholds default to `0.5` and `0.25`, projection defaults to
`false`, the Warbler ramp distance defaults to `12.0`, and the Watch window is
hidden by default. Creating deviation vectors or an unmeasured group and
limiting each point to one measurement all default off.

new instrument was added, and its identity when applicable. Tolerance and
minimum point count default to `0`; matching each measurement to the closest
reference point defaults to `true`. The installed SA documentation states that
silent mode starts measurement automatically but does not suppress this
particular dialog, so Briosa does not promise headless execution.

initial survey group and positional and rotational tolerances are optional.
Zero or omitted tolerances mean that SA applies no tolerance.

<p className="catalog-reference-scope">Reference below: SA 2026.1.0529.7. API links and Briosa defaults apply to that target.</p>

Waits for active trapping to advance before allowing execution to continue. It
returns immediately when trapping is inactive and may otherwise block
indefinitely. A client deadline or cancellation does not prove that the
underlying SA wait stopped; Briosa never automatically replays this call.

<summary>SA 2024.1.0508.5: Signature and Disposition</summary>

Planned for the 2024 target; implementation and runtime validation are pending.

The MP inputs, outputs, and choices match the 2026 counterpart. Its disposition rationale also applies to 2024.

Collection-object inputs use the [2024 collection/name binding](/mp-command-catalog/2024.1.0508.5/review-notes#collection-object-bindings).

**Captured 2024 Argument Signature**

| Direction | Exact MP Argument | Argument Kind |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Input | `Cloud Name` | Collection Object Name |
| Input | `Surfaces` | Collection Object Name Ref List |
| Input | `Maximum Coarse CAD Mesh Edge Length` | Double |
| Input | `Use Fine CAD Mesh (50% of Coarse)?` | Boolean |
| Input | `Execute Alignment?` | Boolean |
| Output | `RMS Deviation` | Double |
| Output | `Average Deviation` | Double |
| Output | `Maximum Absolute Deviation` | Double |
| Output | `Resultant Transform in Working Frame` | Transform |

<p className="catalog-reference-scope">Reference below: SA 2026.1.0529.7. API links and Briosa defaults apply to that target.</p>

Computes cloud-to-surface alignment and returns RMS, average, and maximum
absolute deviations plus the resulting transform in the Working frame. Maximum
coarse mesh edge length defaults to `0.0`, fine mesh to `false`, and applying
the alignment to `true`. SA clamps mesh lengths below 5 mm to 5 mm. When
application is disabled, callers can use the returned analytical transform.

| Direction | Exact MP Argument | Argument Kind |

Initiates synchronized measurement between a master and slave instrument. The
slave-group suffix defaults to `_Slave`, locating one instrument defaults to
`true`, locating the master rather than the slave defaults to `false`, and
waiting for completion defaults to `true`. A deadline or cancellation does not
prove that an in-progress SA measurement stopped.

in the Working frame, and measured TCP points. The result preserves solution
validity, the refined transform, both six-component uncertainty vectors, RMS
and maximum errors, goodness of fit, robustness, and SA's result notes.

contract.

Planned for the 2024 target; implementation and runtime validation are pending.

The MP inputs, outputs, and choices match the 2026 counterpart. Its disposition rationale also applies to 2024.

**Captured 2024 Argument Signature**

| Direction | Exact MP Argument | Argument Kind |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Input | `Measurements` | Point Name Ref List |

</details>

<p className="catalog-reference-scope">Reference below: SA 2026.1.0529.7. API links and Briosa defaults apply to that target.</p>

Constructs SA uncertainty ellipsoids for the supplied measured points. The
displayed uncertainty combines each measurement's uncertainty with its
instrument base uncertainty relative to the WORLD frame. SA reports failure
when points are missing or do not contain uncertainty data.

Sets the selected WRTL instrument's integer channel. Channel defaults to `0`.
Briosa adds no range validation because the available exact-target evidence
does not establish valid channel limits.
